Reads the plan + the GitHub issue + the codebase fresh and returns a verdict (SOLID / REVISE / RETHINK) plus a findings list to the caller. Invoked as `@plan-reviewer &lt;plan-file-path&gt;` — between the architect's draft and the first commit. NEVER reads prior planning discussion or any earlier review of the plan — a fresh set of eyes is the whole point. ([Sfzmango/Maungs-agentic-toolbelt](https://github.com/Sfzmango/Maungs-agentic-toolbelt/blob/HEAD/agents/plan-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **plan-reviewer**: Reviews development plans thoroughly before implementation to identify potential issues, missing considerations, or better alternatives. Perfect for high-risk changes like migrations, integrations, or architectural changes. ([Tonksthebear/trybotster](https://github.com/Tonksthebear/trybotster/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/plan-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **plan-reviewer**: Reviews plans for requirement coverage. Independent validation. ([BryceEWatson/bryce-labs-toolkit](https://github.com/BryceEWatson/bryce-labs-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/spec-workflow/agents/plan-reviewer.md))
- **plan-reviewer**: Opus-powered implementation plan reviewer. Validates a writing-plans artifact before execution begins — checks spec coverage, task granularity, Karpathy anti-patterns, design principle violations, placeholder presence, type consistency, and verification step quality. Produces a structured PASS/FAIL report. Invoked by writing-plans skill after the plan is written and before execution is handed off. ([CagesThrottleUs/private-ai-harness](https://github.com/CagesThrottleUs/private-ai-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/plan-reviewer.md))
- **plan-reviewer**: Plannerが作成した計画の妥当性を検証し、リスクや改善点を指摘する専門エージェント。 計画書と仕様書を照合し、実現可能性・完全性・リスクを評価する。 ([DIO0550/d-market-orchestrator](https://github.com/DIO0550/d-market-orchestrator/blob/HEAD/plugins/copilot-chat-orchestrator-plugin/agents/plan-reviewer.md))
- **plan-reviewer**: 実装計画を検証して指摘だけを返す。implementation-flow のフェーズ 4 から呼ぶ。計画（変更するファイル / テストケース / 却下案 / 未決の判断）を渡すと、帰属先・依存方向・型の境界・テストの十分さを rules/ と照らして検証する。実装・ファイルの変更は行わない。 ([DIO0550/design-composer](https://github.com/DIO0550/design-composer/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/plan-reviewer.md))
- **plan-reviewer**: Independent second reader of a plan before execution (gate G7). Reviews goal-backward — will executing this plan achieve the stated goal? — and gives the frozen contract block an adversarial abuse-lens read. Use after the orchestrator drafts a milestone/unit plan and before any coding agent is briefed; mandatory for security- or architecture-critical units. ([Haadhi76/workflow](https://github.com/Haadhi76/workflow/blob/HEAD/orchestrated-workflow/agents/plan-reviewer.md))
- **plan-reviewer**: 実装計画をレビューし approve/reject を判定する。コードの変更はしない。 ([Hogeyama/nix-agent-sandbox](https://github.com/Hogeyama/nix-agent-sandbox/blob/HEAD/skills/gated-commit-loop/agents/plan-reviewer.md))
